#: The four keys T014 adds to a Foundation document. Exported so that both
#: parsers extend their own allowlist from one place: a key allowed by one
#: parser and not the other would be a document the template store accepts and
#: the Site store refuses.
FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS = frozenset(
    {
        "topology",
        "devices",
        "signal_mappings",
        "control_assumptions",
        "site_controls",
    }
)

TOPOLOGY_KEYS = frozenset({"nodes", "connections"})
TOPOLOGY_NODE_KEYS = frozenset({"node_id", "component_id", "node_role"})
CONNECTION_KEYS = frozenset({"connection_id", "from_node", "to_node", "medium"})
DEVICE_KEYS = frozenset(
    {"device_id", "device_type", "display_name", "component_id", "signals"}
)
SIGNAL_KEYS = frozenset({"signal_id", "display_name", "unit"})
SIGNAL_MAPPING_KEYS = frozenset(
    {"mapping_id", "device_id", "signal_id", "component_id"}
)
CONTROL_ASSUMPTION_KEYS = frozenset(
    {"assumption_id", "display_name", "component_id", "basis", "statement"}
)
#: The keys one typed component property may declare. `kind` is absent by
#: design: it is the vocabulary's answer, not the document's, so there is no
#: position a document can write a second one in.
COMPONENT_PROPERTY_KEYS = frozenset(
    {"property_key", "value", "unit", "source", "source_version"}
)

# Cardinality bounds. A document that declares ten thousand connections is
# refused rather than rendered: a screen nobody can scroll past is the same
"""Domain records for Site configuration templates and for Sites.

Two identity spaces live here and they never merge. A template has
`template_id` and `template_version`, no `site_id`, no lifecycle status, no
location, and no place-bound timezone; it cannot be listed as a Site, targeted
by a scenario, simulated, or receive evidence. A Site has `site_id` and nothing
else is its identity - not its display name, not the template it came from, and
not the shell it was created in.

Neither record type is a base, mode, or parameterization of the other, and the
Site records below repeat rather than reuse the template component shape,
because a Site's Foundation is a copy taken at creation time.

The Foundation content on both is component truth declared by a document. It is
not evidence: that a template or a Site declares a 100 kW PV array is not a
claim that any array exists, is commissioned, or has ever reported a
measurement.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

# The M1 Site types. A template declares which kind of Site it would produce;
# that is a statement about the archetype, not about any Site.
SITE_TYPES = frozenset({"MINIGRID", "COLDCHAIN"})

# Component archetypes a template may declare. Strict and closed: an
# unsupported component type is a configuration error, not a passthrough
# string, because later slices bind topology, devices, and signal mappings to
# these values.
COMPONENT_TYPES = frozenset(
    {
        "PV_ARRAY",
        "INVERTER",
        "BATTERY",
        "POWER_CONVERSION_SYSTEM",
        "GENERATOR",
        "FUEL_TANK",
        "AC_BUS",
        "LOAD",
        "COLD_ROOM",
        "METER",
        # W2-01 (`D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` A): the cold-chain equipment.
        "TEMPERATURE_PROBE",
        "COMPRESSOR",
        "TRANSFER_SWITCH",
    }
)

# Units a declared design rating may use. Also closed, for the same reason:
# a canonical unit vocabulary is what later telemetry mapping is checked
# against.
#
# W2-01 adds `degC` for an actual declared temperature rating only; the cold
# thermal facts are typed properties, never a rating (`D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` C).
RATING_UNITS = frozenset({"kW", "kWh", "kVA", "V", "A", "Hz", "L", "degC"})

# --- Typed component properties ---------------------------------------------
#
# T020A adds the second thing a Foundation may say about a component. A
# `Rating` is the one nameplate magnitude a component was sold with; a
# property is one named, typed, unit-carrying physical or control fact about
# that same component, and a component may declare several.
#
# The vocabulary below is CLOSED, in the same way `RATING_UNITS` and
# `SIGNAL_UNITS` are closed and for the same reason: a model profile binds to
# a property by name, so a document that could coin its own property keys
# could coin a key no profile will ever look for, and a run would block on a
# value that looks declared. A slice that needs a new property adds it here.
#
# The unit is the vocabulary's, not the author's. Specific fuel consumption is
# `L/kWh` because that is the machine's own property
# (`D-2026-09-22-consumption-coefficient-unit`); a document declaring it in
# `L/h` is declaring a number that is only true at one operating point, and
# the parser refuses it rather than storing it and letting a later consumer
# find out.

#: What kind of fact a property is. `PHYSICAL` is something the machine is;
#: `CONTROL` is a configured control value about it.
#:
#: `CONTROL` here is an inspectable typed property and nothing more. It is not
#: a controller, not a setpoint a run may write, and not a Site-scoped
#: Controls capability: the first controller that CONSUMES one of these
#: arrives with T024, and the T016 restriction on control-state vocabulary is
#: untouched - no switching position, no breaker state, no operating mode.
COMPONENT_PROPERTY_KINDS = frozenset({"PHYSICAL", "CONTROL"})

#: Where a declared property came from. Same two values, and the same meaning,
#: as `CONTROL_ASSUMPTION_BASES`: `TEMPLATE` is a property the archetype
#: declared and the Site copied, `SITE` is one declared for this Site.
COMPONENT_PROPERTY_SOURCES = frozenset({"TEMPLATE", "SITE"})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PropertyDefinition:
    """One member of the closed property vocabulary.

    The unit belongs to the definition rather than to the document: a property
    key names a quantity, and a quantity has one canonical unit here for the
    same reason a scenario parameter does.
    """

    unit: str
    kind: str
    display_name: str


#: The closed property vocabulary. Four members, which is what this slice's
#: minimum physical proof and its component-control demonstration need.
#:
#: `tank-capacity` and `specific-fuel-consumption` are the physical pair the
#: first causal kernel reads out of a frozen run. `reserve-state-of-charge`
#: and `minimum-runtime` are the control pair: typed, inspectable, and
#: consumed by nothing in this build.

 Decision `D-2026-10-18-w2-05-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-05a-ev-foundation.md` against current main after merged W2-00..03; the build branch must receive W2-04's `0023` before creating `0024`. Authority: amended `.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md`, `D-2026-10-18-w2-mockup-authority`, `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec`. EV mock content and layout are binding. The cited symbols, rather than older design line numbers, are current code anchors.
 
+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
+
 ## Readiness gate (checked 2026-10-18)
 
 - [x] Identifier and wire formats: UUID Site/asset/revision IDs, UTC effective selections, v4 session/connector identity, half-open schedule intervals and exact EV roles below.

Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
+
 ## Readiness gate (checked 2026-10-18)
 
 - [x] Identifier and wire formats: UUID Site/asset/revision IDs, UTC effective selections, v4 session/connector identity, half-open schedule intervals and exact EV roles below.

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

+## Architect implementer dry-read (2026-10-18; governs ambiguous wording below)
+
+Use five reviewable commits, one builder run and handoff each: **1 strict six-scenario catalog/frozen identity and EV Lab choice; 2 closed simulator pack and six-case numerical tests; 3 public transform/v4 mapping and gateway Stage; 4 persisted EV artifact, delayed schedule, restart and Commit; 5 SELECT-only replay/manifests/final guards.** 05a's reviewed `EV-LAB-001` fixture, role matrix, effective Foundation/binding IDs and `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile are inputs. 05b must not recreate the fixture or change 0024. No 05b migration is required.
+
+**Identity and registration questions.** The fixture Site code is `EV-LAB-001`, Site timezone `UTC`, gateway `GW-EV-LAB-01`, feed `feed-1`, meter `site-meter-1`, charger `charger-1`, connectors `c1,c2`. Seven exact device/signal tuples per connector and the Site import tuple are in 05a's Q1 answer. Do not emit an unsuffixed `charger-controller/session-event`; those retained W2-00 table entries serve compatibility tests only. The `charger-1` component supplies the common 60 kW rating and parent relation, while c1/c2 each have their own 60 kW limit and meter. `source_id` comes from registration; never synthesize it from device ID. In `gateway/mappings.py` register the EV profile as a distinct closed mapping that uses precisely these tuples and the published 05a binding revision. Report `connector_id` is the bound c1/c2 asset ID, while `session_id` is the deterministic per-run scoped session token, never the charger/component ID. Snapshot role, channel, unit and metadata must agree before Draft; wrong source/asset/tuple/profile yields a typed binding refusal, not a default to cold.
+
+**Fixture timing and profile questions.** 05a authors/imports the fixture and publishes its first EV binding revision. 05b selects the existing complete Foundation/binding revisions effective for the 6 October window; the earlier 1 October import/publish timestamps describe that basis and must not be replayed as new revisions. The scenario document stores UTC window, minute step, seed 20517 and exact versioned `ev-charger/1`, `ev-publication/1`, `ev-observation/1`. These are the only selectable values: Lab shows them read-only, rejects a request that differs, and never accepts free-form private forcing. Canonical scenario SHA hashes its strict parsed JSON object with sorted keys, decimal strings and UTC `Z` timestamps; observation SHA hashes the ordered parsed observation rows as specified below. Frozen identity adds those SHAs only for EV; old run fingerprints and artifact serialization remain byte-for-byte the same.
+
+**Scenario and output questions.** The six exact force/observation case tables in `.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md` §3 are normative, including event order and expected numerics; no implementer-chosen seventh event or alternate charging curve. A per-minute private document uses UTC boundary, both connector states/session IDs/requested and allocated kW, site available/import kW, cumulative exact kWh, active fault and forcing ordinal. Sort c1 before c2 at equal boundary, then the design's PLUG-before-FAULT tie rule. Build a deterministic private digest from those 61 boundary documents; only the digest and terminal projection enter the persisted run record. Public state reports are transition plus each minute, with identical same-boundary state deduplicated; power/energy means and deltas cover aligned half-open intervals with split subintervals at session boundaries. Every emitted channel is one of 05a's tuples. Stage admission happens before terminal artifact/schedule is committed; a refused Stage is FAILED, cannot Commit, and retains its refusal code.
+
+**Late, cutoff and replay questions.** `source_time` is the observed transition or interval end; normal `published_at=source_time+3s`. The held late END retains its source time, event ID, report key and payload while release is 09:05 UTC. No first-cutoff success is fabricated from the future report. The selected replay read is exact published evidence for the named run at the request's database snapshot, not a live diagnostic; `receipts` distinguish duplicate, rejected and accepted ordinals. `public_digest` hashes the canonical disposition tuples already specified, in persisted batch order then ordinal order, with a literal null accepted ID when absent. The empty replay hashes canonical `[]`. A second clean replay may use new database UUIDs: compare canonical public report bytes, envelope/batch SHA and disposition shape, while manifest fixture UUID/accepted IDs are pinned for the first controlled fixture database. Do not claim UUID equality across independent databases.
+

Preserve the Lab's existing Site selector, scenario-choice rows, read-only window/seed/profile fields, Draft/Stage/Commit controls, status chips, empty-state area and error/retry placement. The six choices come only from `GET /api/simulator-lab/ev-scenarios?site_id=...`; a 403 is a scoped refusal in the same panel, 404 is unknown Site, an empty list says `No EV test runs are available for this Site`, and 503 says `EV test runs are temporarily unavailable` with retry. The Site financial `ev-scenarios` mock route is a separate unavailable Financials surface and is not populated by this Lab catalog. No Site EV panel is added in 05b.
+
+**Seam/guard questions.** Extend only the closed profile registries and named host execution branches; `host/lab_app.py` injects an EV input port whose implementation re-reads selected Foundation and scenario by exact ID/SHA. The pure pack/public transform imports no SQL, HTTP or accepted-evidence module. Gateway remains the v4 sequence/message/outbox writer, `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` remains accepted/disposition writer, and EV replay uses SELECTs only. `FROZEN_RUN_NOT_RECONSTRUCTIBLE` applies to missing/changed frozen inputs before Stage; unknown schema/profile/document keys refuse before Draft; damaged persisted artifact is `ARTIFACT_UNREADABLE`; Stage publication refusal is `PUBLICATION_REFUSED` with the gateway's underlying ordinal codes recorded. A run that is terminal remains terminal after restart. Rule-8 table owners and SQL-driver import allowlists gain no EV exemption.

 Decision `D-2026-10-18-w2-05-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-05a-ev-foundation.md` against current main after merged W2-00..03; the build branch must receive W2-04's `0023` before creating `0024`. +## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
+

+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.

Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.

No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
+

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
