+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

# --- requests ------------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BindingRequest:
    device_id: str
    signal_id: str
    asset_id: str | None
    role: str
    source_unit: str
    canonical_unit: str
    conversion: dict[str, Any]

    @staticmethod
    def from_wire(item: Any, index: int) -> "BindingRequest":
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise BindingRefused("BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID", f"bindings[{index}] must be an object.", status=422)
        unknown, missing = set(item) - BINDING_ITEM_KEYS, BINDING_ITEM_KEYS - set(item)
        if unknown or missing:
            raise BindingRefused(
                "BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID",
                f"bindings[{index}]: unknown fields {sorted(unknown)}; missing fields {sorted(missing)}.",
                status=422,
            )
        for key in ("device_id", "signal_id", "role", "source_unit", "canonical_unit"):
            if not isinstance(item[key], str) or not item[key]:
                raise BindingRefused(
                    "BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID", f"bindings[{index}].{key} must be non-empty text.", status=422
                )
        if item["asset_id"] is not None and not isinstance(item["asset_id"], str):
            raise BindingRefused(
                "BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID", f"bindings[{index}].asset_id must be a UUID or null.", status=422
            )
        return BindingRequest(
            device_id=item["device_id"],
            signal_id=item["signal_id"],
            asset_id=item["asset_id"],
            role=item["role"],
            source_unit=item["source_unit"],
            canonical_unit=item["canonical_unit"],
            conversion=item["conversion"],
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeclaredSignal:
                for row in foundation:
                    first = critical_load_code(row.content)
                    if first != asset.asset_code:
                        problem(
                            "BINDING_CRITICAL_LOAD_MISMATCH", index,
                            f"{role.label} must name the first load of the critical-load priority in Foundation "
                            f"v{row.version} ({first or 'not configured'}), not {asset.asset_code}.",
                        )
            if role.role == "battery_soc" and asset is not None:
                # The state of charge and the reserve control must describe one battery.
                for row in foundation:
                    reserve = battery_reserve(row.content)
                    if reserve is not None and reserve["asset_code"] != asset.asset_code:
                        problem(
                            "BINDING_BATTERY_MISMATCH", index,
                            f"{role.label} names {asset.asset_code}, but the battery reserve in Foundation "
                            f"v{row.version} is configured on {reserve['asset_code']}.",
                        )
            key = (role.role, req.asset_id if role.cardinality == "PER_ASSET" else None)
            if key in single:
                problem(
                    "BINDING_ROLE_CONFLICT", index,
                    f"{role.label} is already bound by bindings[{single[key]}]"
                    + (" for this asset." if key[1] else "."),
                )
            single.setdefault(key, index)
        if source is not None and gateways:
            rows.append(
                BindingRow(
                    id=uuid4(),
                    gateway_id=gateways[0].id,
                    source_id=source.id,
                    device_id=req.device_id,
                    signal_id=req.signal_id,
                    asset_id=None if asset is None else asset.id,
                    role=req.role,
                    source_unit=req.source_unit,
                    canonical_unit=req.canonical_unit,
                    conversion=req.conversion,
                    v4_mapping=v4_mapping,
                )
            )
    if problems:
        first = problems[0]
        raise BindingRefused(
            first["code"],
            "The binding set was refused and nothing was written: "
            + " ".join(p["message"] for p in problems),
            status=422,
            problems=problems,
        )
    return tuple(rows)


# --- planning --------------------------------------------------------------------


def _with_signal(content: Mapping[str, Any], signal: DeclaredSignal) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """`content` with the signal declared on its device, or None if already declared."""
    updated = json.loads(json.dumps(content))
    for device in updated.get("devices") or ():
        if device["device_id"] != signal.device_id:
            continue
        for existing in device.get("signals") or ():
            if existing["signal_id"] == signal.signal_id:
                if existing["unit"] != signal.unit:
                    raise BindingRefused(
                        "BINDING_UNIT_MISMATCH",
                        f"{signal.device_id} / {signal.signal_id} is already declared in "
                        f"{existing['unit']!r}, not {signal.unit!r}.",
                        status=422,
                    )
                return None
        device["signals"] = [
            *(device.get("signals") or []),
            {"signal_id": signal.signal_id, "display_name": signal.display_name, "unit": signal.unit},
        ]
        return updated
    raise BindingRefused(
        "BINDING_SOURCE_UNKNOWN",
        f"This Site's Foundation declares no device {signal.device_id!r}.",

 Decision `D-2026-10-18-w2-05-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-05a-ev-foundation.md` against current main after merged W2-00..03; the build branch must receive W2-04's `0023` before creating `0024`. +## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
+

Every emitted channel is one of 05a's tuples. Stage admission happens before terminal artifact/schedule is committed; a refused Stage is FAILED, cannot Commit, and retains its refusal code.

The empty replay hashes canonical `[]`. A second clean replay may use new database UUIDs: compare canonical public report bytes, envelope/batch SHA and disposition shape, while manifest fixture UUID/accepted IDs are pinned for the first controlled fixture database. +**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these seven devices per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`), and reject a binding/requested cadence greater than the selected property's seconds or above its role ceiling (`BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`). `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded and validated for the selected binding clock tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with different effective periods; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site with its own later effective period. No new Site codes are introduced, and the six 05b replay cases use the complete basis only. Each test pins `at` and revision IDs; a revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.

+2. **Q2:** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` for missing connector, feed/limit, required meter, session event or status (`CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`). `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND`, `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED` are nonblocking messages under `READY_TO_REPORT`. Missing optional fault, power, requested or allocated channels has no readiness code; its dependent capability stays unavailable.
+3. **Q3:** Only absent feed/connector or `ev-site-import-limit` is publishable as a configuration gap. A declared charger or connector missing any of its eight required physical/control/cadence/clock properties refuses Foundation publication as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` with property path; so do malformed values/topology/schedule. There is no extra readiness code for such an unpublished revision.
+4. **Q4:** State cadence is the connector `ev-status-cadence` in minutes converted to seconds, >0 and ≤60 s. Energy, power, import, requested and allocated use `ev-meter-cadence`, >0 and ≤300 s; EV publication's interval grid is aligned 300 s. Events have null cadence. Above-ceiling Foundation values refuse `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`; a binding cadence above its selected property/role ceiling refuses `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. Positive `ev-clock-error-bound` seconds is selected binding tolerance, displayed but not observed clock attestation.
+5. **Q5:** `connector_enable_schedules` is a new top-level Foundation key, added to shared `FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS`. Exactly one entry per connector; empty weekly array means no enabled hours. Fixture Site timezone is `UTC`, both entries recur TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 at offset 0 over their revision's effective interval. Check every occurrence for bounded revisions; an open-ended revision admits only a zone with fixed future offset, so a DST zone needs bounded revisions. Missing entry, overlap, invalid/ambiguous/nonexistent local endpoint refuses `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`.
+6. **Q6:** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and complete binding registration. All missing-meter/event/status and limit test examples are later effective revisions of the **same** `EV-LAB-001`, never additional Site codes. Missing role uses a binding revision; missing limit uses a Foundation revision. 05b reuses the complete 05a basis and adds six scenario documents.
+7. **Q7:** Reserve `0024_w2_ev_config` for 05a. Proceed with nonmigration commits now; wait for W2-04's reviewed `0023` on the 05a branch before writing 0024, whose `down_revision` is 0023. 05b has no migration.
+8. **Q8:** `ev_connector_energy` is `INTERVAL_DELTA`/kWh with parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` from kWh only; its connector meter entry requires a session. Other EV interval roles are NUMBER/`IDENTITY` in kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`. EV state is STATE/`ENUM`, events EVENT/`IDENTITY`. Bind the fixture under distinct `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1`; preserve cold profile/version and historical bytes. +## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these three devices and seven channels per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field: derive `bindings[].cadence_seconds` only from the selected Foundation property. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`); reject any future submitted cadence field as `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`, not an invented `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded as the selected configured tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these three devices and seven channels per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field: derive `bindings[].cadence_seconds` only from the selected Foundation property. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`); reject any future submitted cadence field as `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`, not an invented `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded as the selected configured tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with nonoverlapping effective periods after the 6 October complete-run window; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site. Tests use isolated stores/DBs and pin `at` and revision IDs, so 05b's complete 6 October basis is untouched. No new Site codes are introduced. A revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
+

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these three devices and seven channels per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field: derive `bindings[].cadence_seconds` only from the selected Foundation property. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`); reject any future submitted cadence field as `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`, not an invented `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded as the selected configured tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with nonoverlapping effective periods after the 6 October complete-run window; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site. Tests use isolated stores/DBs and pin `at` and revision IDs, so 05b's complete 6 October basis is untouched. No new Site codes are introduced. A revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these three devices and seven channels per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field: derive `bindings[].cadence_seconds` only from the selected Foundation property. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`); reject any future submitted cadence field as `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`, not an invented `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded as the selected configured tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with nonoverlapping effective periods after the 6 October complete-run window; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site. Tests use isolated stores/DBs and pin `at` and revision IDs, so 05b's complete 6 October basis is untouched. No new Site codes are introduced. A revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.

+**Scenario and output questions.** The six exact force/observation case tables in `.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md` §3 are normative, including event order and expected numerics; no implementer-chosen seventh event or alternate charging curve. A per-minute private document uses UTC boundary, both connector states/session IDs/requested and allocated kW, site available/import kW, cumulative exact kWh, active fault and forcing ordinal. Sort c1 before c2 at equal boundary, then the design's PLUG-before-FAULT tie rule. Build a deterministic private digest from those 61 boundary documents; only the digest and terminal projection enter the persisted run record. Public state reports are transition plus each minute, with identical same-boundary state deduplicated; power/energy means and deltas cover aligned half-open intervals with split subintervals at session boundaries. Every emitted channel is one of 05a's tuples. Stage admission happens before terminal artifact/schedule is committed; a refused Stage is FAILED, cannot Commit, and retains its refusal code.
+
+**Late, cutoff and replay questions.** `source_time` is the observed transition or interval end; normal `published_at=source_time+3s`. The held late END retains its source time, event ID, report key and payload while release is 09:05 UTC. No first-cutoff success is fabricated from the future report. The selected replay read is exact published evidence for the named run at the request's database snapshot, not a live diagnostic; `receipts` distinguish duplicate, rejected and accepted ordinals. `public_digest` hashes the canonical disposition tuples already specified, in persisted batch order then ordinal order, with a literal null accepted ID when absent. The empty replay hashes canonical `[]`. A second clean replay may use new database UUIDs: compare canonical public report bytes, envelope/batch SHA and disposition shape, while manifest fixture UUID/accepted IDs are pinned for the first controlled fixture database. Do not claim UUID equality across independent databases.
+

The six choices come only from `GET /api/simulator-lab/ev-scenarios?site_id=...`; a 403 is a scoped refusal in the same panel, 404 is unknown Site, an empty list says `No EV test runs are available for this Site`, and 503 says `EV test runs are temporarily unavailable` with retry. The Site financial `ev-scenarios` mock route is a separate unavailable Financials surface and is not populated by this Lab catalog. No Site EV panel is added in 05b.

+**Seam/guard questions.** Extend only the closed profile registries and named host execution branches; `host/lab_app.py` injects an EV input port whose implementation re-reads selected Foundation and scenario by exact ID/SHA. The pure pack/public transform imports no SQL, HTTP or accepted-evidence module. +**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field: derive `bindings[].cadence_seconds` only from the selected Foundation property. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`); reject any future submitted cadence field as `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`, not an invented `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded as the selected configured tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+

Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.

A revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.

+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. +**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.

For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.

+1. **Q1:** Keep `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`. Events have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field; derive the selected read's `cadence_seconds` from Foundation. Above-ceiling Foundation values refuse `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`; an unexpected request cadence field refuses `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`. Positive `ev-clock-error-bound` seconds is selected configured tolerance, not observed clock attestation.
+5. **Q5:** `connector_enable_schedules` is a new top-level Foundation key, added to shared `FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS`. Exactly one entry per connector; empty weekly array means no enabled hours. Fixture Site timezone is `UTC`, both entries recur TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 at offset 0 over their revision's effective interval. Check every occurrence for bounded revisions; an open-ended revision admits only a zone with fixed future offset, so a DST zone needs bounded revisions. Missing entry, overlap, invalid/ambiguous/nonexistent local endpoint refuses `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`.

+## Architect dry-read resolution (2026-10-18; governs any earlier ambiguous wording)
+
+Build in reviewable commits: **1 vocabulary, parser, typed schedule and validator; 2 EV roles, closed v4 channel matrix, binding publication/snapshot and fixture; 3 selected API; 4 three UI routes and visual proof; 5 migration and final guards/tests.** Commit 5 may be prepared only after W2-04 `0023` is merged into this branch. No interim EV migration is permitted; `0024_w2_ev_config` is reserved for 05a. Run each commit's focused checks and hand off before the next builder run.
+
+**Q1, connector identity.** `BINDING_CHANNEL_OVERLAP` and `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` stay unchanged. In EV-LAB-001 the c1 tuples are `(c1-charger-controller,session-event)`, `(c1-connector-controller,fault-event|state|requested-power|allocated-limit)`, `(c1-connector-meter,energy|power)`; c2 uses the identical signal names with `c2-` device prefixes. Each of these three devices and seven channels per connector has `component_id=c1` or `c2` respectively and binds to that same EV_CONNECTOR asset. `(site-meter,import)` has `component_id=site-meter-1`, binds to its METER asset, and is the sole Site import channel. `charger-1` is topology and ratings only; it owns no connector channel. Each tuple is a distinct exact entry in `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`; no prefix wildcard or runtime-generated accepted tuple. Keep the two old unsuffixed W2-00 reserved tuples for their existing tests, label their roles, but do not bind them in this fixture. The v4 snapshot metadata must come from the exact entry. S2's existing one-binding connector check continues to compare the envelope connector asset ID to c1 or c2.
+
+**Q2, readiness.** `CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE` applies to `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, or `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`; `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND` is a nonblocking capability message under `READY_TO_REPORT`, as are `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED` and `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. Missing optional fault, connector power, requested power or allocated limit yields no readiness code; the exact capability that needs it stays unavailable. Required energy, event and state bindings are per declared connector. A configured Site may be ready while source contact and commissioning remain unknown.
+
+**Q3, absent properties.** Missing `ev-site-import-limit` on an otherwise valid feed is the sole publishable numeric gap and emits `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`; absence of feed or all connectors is likewise publishable as configuration-incomplete so the selected read can explain it. For every *declared* charger/connector, missing `ev-charger-max-power`, `ev-connector-max-power`, `ev-connector-enabled`, `ev-meter-resolution`, `ev-meter-error-bound`, `ev-status-cadence`, `ev-meter-cadence` or `ev-clock-error-bound` refuses Foundation publication with `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` and a path to that property. Present wrong type/unit, nonfinite/out-of-range value, invalid topology or schedule uses the same typed refusal. Do not pretend a missing physical or clock parameter is a readiness finding. A Site with no declared connector cannot publish a binding set or Draft a Lab run.
+
+**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. Event channels have null cadence. `BindingRequest` has no cadence field: derive `bindings[].cadence_seconds` only from the selected Foundation property. Reject a property above its ceiling at Foundation publication (`EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`); reject any future submitted cadence field as `BINDING_REQUEST_INVALID`, not an invented `BINDING_CADENCE_INVALID`. `ev-clock-error-bound` is positive seconds and is recorded as the selected configured tolerance; 05a makes no observed clock-attestation claim. No cadence is inferred from observed timestamps.
+
+**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (UTC qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.
+
+**Q6, fixtures.** 05a creates `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/ev-lab-001.yaml` and registers the Site in its focused test/setup path. There is one Site, `EV-LAB-001`: the stocked Foundation and published binding revision are its first complete basis. Missing meter/event/status examples are separate *test-created binding revisions* on that same Site, with nonoverlapping effective periods after the 6 October complete-run window; the missing-limit example is a later Foundation revision on the same Site. Tests use isolated stores/DBs and pin `at` and revision IDs, so 05b's complete 6 October basis is untouched. No new Site codes are introduced. A revision never rewrites the original. 05b adds scenarios and uses this fixture, not a second copy.
+
+**Q7, migration order.** W2-04 owns `0023`; 05a reserves `0024_w2_ev_config` now. Vocabulary/API/UI commits may proceed before 0023, but do not generate or test 0024 against 0022. Merge reviewed 0023 into the 05a branch, create `0024` with `down_revision` pointing to 0023, then run upgrade/downgrade and final tests. 05b adds no migration.
+
+**Q8, kinds and profile.** `ev_connector_energy` has role `value_kind=INTERVAL_DELTA`, source/canonical unit kWh, parameter-free `IDENTITY_KWH` that accepts only kWh. Other EV interval roles use `value_kind=NUMBER`, `IDENTITY`, kW and v4 `INTERVAL_MEAN`; STATE uses `ENUM`, and EVENT uses `IDENTITY`. `(cX-connector-meter,energy)` requires nonnull session and connector in v4; power and controller interval channels require connector and carry session when a session exists; Site import has explicit-null session/connector. Preserve existing `V4_MAPPING_PROFILE_ID=simulator-lab-gateway-cold` for cold and its historical snapshots; EV binds under the new exact `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` profile, with an explicit profile-to-closed-table lookup. Do not mutate cold profile/version or v3 bytes. 05b registers the producer for that EV profile.
